10a1(dkon brtsegs): @#/ O /ste/tha ba zhe sdang slangs nas pha mthar sgrol//ji ltar sangs rgyas mchog gi gzi mtha' yas//mi dbang gzi rjid phyogs bcur lam mer gda'//de ltar bdag kyang sangs 
10a2(dkon brtsegs): rgyas chos rgyal gyur//skye dgu dga' dang mchi las rab thar bgyi//sbyin dang dul dang tshul khrims bzod//brtson 'grus bsam gtan ting 'dzin rab mchog//brtul shugs 'di dag bdag gis
10a3(dkon brtsegs): yang dag blang//sems can skyob pa sangs rgyas gyur par bgyi//byang chub zhi mchog mtshungs med tshol bgyid cing//sangs rgyas bye ba rgya stong du ma'i phrag//gang gi bye ma lta bur mtha' yas pa//
10a4(dkon brtsegs): mgon po de kun bdag gis mchod par bgyi//gang ga'i rdul dang mnyam pa'i 'jig rten khams//de bas gong du mtha' yas zhing gi rnams//de dag kun tu 'od ni rab 'byin pa//'di
10a5(dkon brtsegs): 'dra'i brtson 'grus stobs ni rtsam par bgyi//bdag gis zhing yangs gartso ba dam pa mchog//'dus byas 'dir ni rgyan pa bgyis nasu//mtshungs pa ma mchis mya ngan 'das bde//de
10a6(dkon brtsegs): yang ma mchis pas ni rnam par sbyang//phyogs bcu dag nas lhags pa'i sems can rnams//der lhags myur du bde ba dga' bar 'gyur//sangs rgyas chad ma 'di na bdag gi dbang//ma 
10a7(dkon brtsegs): log brtson 'grus stobs ni bskyed par mtshal//phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten mi chags mkhyen pa can//mkhas pa de dag tu ni bdag sems mkhyen//bdag ni mtshams med mchis te rtag 
10a8(dkon brtsegs): gnas bgyi//smon lam stobs las ldog par yang mi 'gyur//kun dga'o de nas dge slong chos kyi 'byung gnas des//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa 'jig rten dbang phyug rgyal 
10b1(dkon brtsegs): po la/tshigasu cad pa 'di dag gis mngon suM du mngon par stod nas 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das bdag ni bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs
10b2(dkon brtsegs): par 'tshang rgya bar 'tshal te//yang dang yang bla na med pa yang dag pa rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed cing//yongasu snga bar bgyid na ci nas kyang bdag myur du bla na med pa yang dag pas rdzogs
10b3(dkon brtsegs): pa'i byang chub tu mngon pa rdzogs par 'tshang brgya ba dang/'jig rten du de bzhin gshegs pa mi mnyam pa dang mnyam par 'gyur ba de lta bu'i chos//bcom ldan 'das kyis bdag la legs
10b4(dkon brtsegs): par stan du gsol//rnam pa gang dag gis bdag gi sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi yon tan gyi rgyan bkod pa phun suM tshogs pa yongasu 'dzin par 'gyur ba'i rnam pa de yang/bcom ldan 'das kyis
10b5(dkon brtsegs): bdag la yongasu rjod du gsol//kun dga'o de skad ces gsol pa dang/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa 'jig rten dbang phyug rgyal po des/dge slong chos kyi 'byung gnas
10b6(dkon brtsegs): de la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//de'i phyir dge slong khyod bdag nyid kyis sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi/yon tan gyi rgyan bkod pa phan su tshogs pa yongasu bzung shig//des gsol pa/
10b7(dkon brtsegs): bcom ldan bdag spro ba ma mchiso//de bzhin gshegs pa gzhan dag gi sangs rgyas kyi zhing go yon tan bkod pa'i rgyan phun sum tshogs pa gang thos nas bdag gi rgyan thams cad yongasu rdzogs
10b8(dkon brtsegs): par 'gyur ba/bcom ldan 'das kyis bshad du gsol//kun dga' de nas de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs 'jig rten dbang phyug rkyal po des/dge slong
11a1(dkon brtsegs): @#////de'i bsam pa mkhyen nas don bzhed pa/phan pa bzhed pa/thugs brtse ba can gis thugs brtse ba nye bar 'thung ste/sangs rgyas kyi tshul mi gcad pa'i phyir sems can rnams la thugs rje chen pa skyed/
11a2(dkon brtsegs): nas la bye ba tham par sangs rgyas bye ba khrag khrig rgya stong phrag brgya bcu rtsa gcig gi sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi yon tan gi rgyan bkad pa phun suM tshogs pa rnam pa dang bcas/yul phyogs dang bcas /gzhi ci las gyur 
11a3(dkon brtsegs): pa dang bcas par yang dag par rab tu bshad do//de nas bcom ldan 'das la /tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bas 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das 'jig rten dbang phyug rgyal po de'i sku tshe'i tshad ni ji tsam 
11a4(dkon brtsegs): zhig thub par gyur//sems can gang dag skyes pa de dag thams cad 'di lta ste/gser gi kha dog tu/kha dog gcig par gyur pa de srid du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon 
11a5(dkon brtsegs): par rdzogs par 'tshang brgya bar mi bgyi'o//bcom ldan 'das gal te bdag gi sangs rgyas kyi zhing de na lha dang mi tha dad pa dag ganga' bar gyur pa gzhan du na kun rdzob kyi tha snyad tsam du ming 
11a6(dkon brtsegs): dang bdas lta dang mi zhes bgyi ba'i grangasu mchi bar ma gyur pa de srid du/bdag bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngan par rdzogs par 'tshang brgya par mi bgyi'o//bcom ldan
11a7(dkon brtsegs): 'das gal te bdag gi sang rgyas kyi zhing der sems can gang dag skyes par gyur pa de dag thams cad gal te tha na sems kyi skad cig thad cig la/sangs rgyas kyi zhing bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong 'ad ba'i 
11a8(dkon brtsegs): rdzu 'phrul la/dbang po'i dam pa'i pha rol tu phyin par ma gyur pa de srid du//bdag bla na med pa yangadag par rdzogs par 'tshang brgya bar mi bgyi'o//bcomaldan 'das gal te bdag gis sangs rgyas 
11b1(dkon brtsegs): kyi zhing der sems can gang dag skyes par gyur pa de dag thams tha na bskal pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong rjesu dran pa'i rtsa bar ma gyur pa de srid du/bdag bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang 
11b2(dkon brtsegs): chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang brgya bar mi bgyi'o//bcom ldan 'das gal te bdag gi sangs rgyas kyi zheng der sems can gang dag/skyes par gyur pa de dag thabs cad tha na 'jig rten gyi khas 
11b3(dkon brtsegs): bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong mthong ba'i lha'i mig mthong ba'i lha'i mig thob par gyur pa de srid du/bdagabla na med pa yang dag pa rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs pa mtshang brgya bar mi bgyi'o //
11b4(dkon brtsegs): bcom ldan 'das gal te ba dag gi sangs rgyas kyi zhing der sems can gangadag skyes par gyur ba de dag thams cad tha na sangs rgyas kyi zhing bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong du cir char chos nyan pa'i 
11b5(dkon brtsegs): lha'i rna ba thob par ma gyur pa de srid du/bdag bla na med pa yang ngag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par 'chang brgya bar mi bgyi'o//bcom ldan 'das gal te/
11b6(dkon brtsegs): bdag gi sangs rgyas kyi zhing dor sems can gang dag skyes pa de dag thams cad tha na/sangs rgyas kyi zhing bye ba khag khrig brgya stong du rtogs pa'i sems can rnams kyi sems kyi spyod pa yongasu
11b7(dkon brtsegs): 'tshal bar /pha rol kyi sems 'tshal ba la mkhas par ma gyur pa de srid du /bdag bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chubamangon par rdzogs par 'tshang brgya bar mi bgyi'o//bcom 
11b8(dkon brtsegs): ldan 'das gal te bdag gi sangs rgyas kyi zhi der sems can gang dag skyed par gyur ba de dag tham cad tha na/rang gi lus la yongasu 'dzin par 'du shes 'ga' zhig skyed na yang de srid du//
13a1(dkon brtsegs): @#/ O /srid du/bdag bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon (   )par rdzogs par 'tshang brgya bar mi bgyi'o//bcom ldan 'das gal te bdag byang chub thob pa'i tshe/sangs rgyas kyi 
13a2(dkon brtsegs): zhing grangs ma mchis dpag tu ma mchis pa dag na /sems can gang dag gis bdag gi ming thos nas sangs rgyas kyi zhing der skye bar bgyi ba'i slad du/sems gtang zhing dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams kyang yongasu sngo bar 
13a3(dkon brtsegs): bgyid na/mtshams ma mchis pa bgyid pa dang /dam pa'i chos spong ba'i sgrib pas bsgribs pa'i sems can rnams ma rtogs par/de dag tha na sems bskyed par 'gyur ba bcus sangs rgyas kyi zhing der
13a4(dkon brtsegs): skye bar ma gyur pa de srid du/bdag bla na med pa yangadag par rdzogs pa'i byang chung mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang brgya bar mi bgyi'i/bcom ldan 'das gal tebadag byang chub thob pa'i cho/

13a7(dkon brtsegs): byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po go cha chen po bgos pa/'jig rten thams cad kyi don du go cha bgos pa/'jig rten tham sa cad kyi don la brtson pa/'jig rten thams cadayongasu mya 
13a8(dkon brtsegs): ngan la zla ba la mngon par brtson pa/'jig rten gi khams thams cad du byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod par 'tshal ba/sangs rgyas thams cad la phud dud bgyi bar 'tshal ba/sems can gang ga'i 
13b1(dkon brtsegs): klung gi bye mo snyed bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub la dgod par 'tshal ba yang gong ma'i spyod pa la mngon du phyogs pa/kun tu bzang po'i spyod pas nges par 'byung ba rnams kyi smon lam kyi khyad 
13b2(dkon brtsegs): par ma rtogs par de dag thams cad bla na med pa yang dag lar rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu skye ba gcig gis thogs par ma gyur pa de srid du/bdag bla na med pa yang dag pa rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon 
13b3(dkon brtsegs): par rdzogs par 'tshang brgya bar mi bgyi'o//bcom ldan 'das gal te bdag byang chub thob pa'i tshe/sangs rgyas kyi zhing der sems can gang dag skyes par gyur pa de dag thams cad snga dro gcig bzhin sangs
13b4(dkon brtsegs): rgyas kyi zhing gzhan dag tu mchis la/sangs rgyas stong phrag mang po dang/sangs rgyas rgya stong phrag mang po dang /sangs rgyas bye ba phrag mang po nas/sangs rgyas bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag mang po dag 
13b5(dkon brtsegs): la 'di lta ste/sangs rgyas kyi mthus bde bar sbyar ba thams cad kyis bsnyen bkur bgyid par ma gyur pa de srid tu/bdag bla na med pa yangadag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang brgya
13b6(dkon brtsegs): bar mi bgyi'o//bcom ldana'adas gal te bdag byang cub thob pa'i tshe/sangs rgyasakyi zhing de na byang chub sos dpa' de dag 'di lta ste/gser ram/dngul lam/nor bu 'am/mu tig gam/bye ru 
13b7(dkon brtsegs): 'am/dung ngam/man shel lam/bye ru 'am/shel lam/spug gam/rdo'i snying po 'am/mu tig dmar po la stsogs pa gang yang rung ba 'am/rin po che thams cad dam/me tog dang/spos dang/
13b8(dkon brtsegs): phreng ba dang/byug pa dang/bdug pa dang/phye ma dang/gos dang/bdugs dang/rgyal mtshan dang/ba dan dang/mar me thams cad dang/klu dang gar dang/rol mo'i dbyangs sgra 'am/rnam pa ji lta bu dag

14a2(dkon brtsegs): par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang brgya bar mi bgyi'o//bcom ldan 'das gal te bdag byang chub thob pa'i tshe/sangs rgyas kyi zhing der sems can gang skyes par gyur pa de

14b1(dkon brtsegs): de dag bzhes par ma gyur pa de srid du/bdag bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par mtshang brgya bar mi bgyi'o//bcom ldan 'das gal te bdag byang chub

14b7(dkon brtsegs): mtho bar mtshal bar ma gyur pa de sring du/bdag bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'chang brgya bar mi bgyi'o//bcom ldan 'das gal te bdag byang

31b1(dkon brtsegs): rkang pa'i rgyan 'di lta bu ste /gcod pan dang /rna ba cha dang /gdu bu dang /dpung rgyan dang /do shal dang /dpung rgyan dang/se mo do dang /na rgyan dang /so rdul dang /gser gyi tsong tshong dang /skags dang /gser gi dri ba dang /mu tig gi dro
31b2(dkon brtsegs): ba dang /rin po che thams cad kyi dril bu g.yer ka'i dra ba dang /rgyan ji lta bu 'dod pa rnam pa de lta bu'i rgyan rin po che brgya stong du mas spras pa dag 'di lta ste /rin po che'i shing ljon pa la 'phyang bas /sangs rgyas kyi zhing de gang bar mthong ste /rgyan

31b4(dkon brtsegs): ba bzhag pa dang ldan pa'i gzhal myed khang ji lta bu 'dod pa de lta bu 'i gzhal myed khang de dag de dag gi mdun du 'byung ste /de dag rin po che'i gzhal myed khang grub pa de dag gi nang na lha'i bu mo bdun stong bdun stong gyis yongs
